### CORRELATION GROUPS

- `2026_05_PHI_JAX::SCORING_UP` (same, strong (qualitative)): game total over, either team total over, QB passing yards over, receiver yards/receptions over, anytime TD yes, total TDs over — These rise and fall together with game scoring and pace.
- `2026_05_PHI_JAX::JAX_SIDE` (same, strong (qualitative)): JAX moneyline, JAX spread, JAX team total over, JAX player props over — Team-side markets share the same game-script risk.
- `2026_05_PHI_JAX::GAME_SCRIPT_OPPOSED` (opposed, moderate (qualitative)): favourite rushing volume over, favourite passing volume over — A team that leads runs more and throws less; volume props on the same team can conflict.
